# Introduction



This is an automated report generated from the opcode run times 
estimation script `./src/estimate_opcode_run_times.py`. The script 
uses data generated by running the 
[EEST benchmark suite](https://github.com/ethereum/execution-spec-tests/tree/main/tests/benchmark) 
with the [Nethermind benchmarking tooling](https://github.com/NethermindEth/gas-benchmarks).

The data includes all the simple opcode tests run between 2025-12-19 and 2025-12-23.

For each opcode and client, an OLS linear regression model is fitted to estimate the 
opcode run time as a function of the opcode count. The results are presented below.

```python
                            OLS Regression Results                            
==============================================================================
Dep. Variable:        run_duration_ms   R-squared:                       0.998

Method:                 Least Squares   F-statistic:                 4.669e+04
Date:                Tue, 23 Dec 2025   Prob (F-statistic):          5.26e-103
Time:                        08:40:43   Log-Likelihood:                -320.84
No. Observations:                  74   AIC:                             645.7
Df Residuals:                      72   BIC:                             650.3
Df Model:                           1                                         
Covariance Type:            nonrobust                                         
==============================================================================
                 coef    std err          t      P>|t|      [0.025      0.975]
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
const         36.4776      4.723      7.723      0.000      27.062      45.894
opcount     2.648e-05   1.23e-07    216.072      0.000    2.62e-05    2.67e-05
==============================================================================
Omnibus:                       31.664   Durbin-Watson:                   1.668
Prob(Omnibus):                  0.000   Jarque-Bera (JB):              270.730
Skew:                          -0.783   Prob(JB):                     1.63e-59
Kurtosis:                      12.239   Cond. No.                     8.36e+07
==============================================================================

Notes:
[1] Standard Errors assume that the covariance matrix of the errors is correctly specified.
[2] The condition number is large, 8.36e+07. This might indicate that there are
strong multicollinearity or other numerical problems.
```
